na varicic coṟkaḷ

na 
nakaram, nakāram eṉpaṉavum itu. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil eṭṭām uyirmeyk kuṟil. melliṉa vakiyil nāṉkām eḻuttu. ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa
nal, nalla eṉṉum poruḷ tarum kuṟil.
e-ṭu:
na + kīraṉ = nakkīraṉ
na + ceḷḷi = nacceḷḷi
na + paṇṇaṉ = nappaṇṇaṉ
nallantuvaṉar, naṉṉākaṉār peyarkaḷ nal eṉṟē niṉṟaṉa.
na maṅkalap poruḷ tarutal civaṉiya vaḻakku. namacivāya.
kuṟilkaḷām no, tu eṉpavi pōla navum poruḷ tarum eṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. iṉi nal eṉpataṉ tokuttalumām.
nakam 
oḷiyuṭiyatum, etiroḷi ceyvatum nakamām. nakutal palloḷi veḷippaṭalāl amintatu.
nakiyē aḻuki	- tol. 1197
nakunayam maṟittal	- tol.120
nakam = mali. katirpaṭṭa aḷavil paṉinīrum paṉippaṭivum iliyum taḷirum pūvum oḷi ceytalāl koṇṭa peyar.
paṉṉakamum nakuveḷḷip paṉivari	- kampa. pāla. 324
nakarap pā (nakarviruttam):
nakara nalaṅ kuṟittup pattu āciriya viruttam pāṭuvatu nakarappāeṉṉum ciṟunūṟ pāṟpaṭum (navanīta. 41).
nakar 
nakartal eṉuñcol virital, akalutal, uyartal mutalām poruḷatu.
virivum uyarvum periya paranta cuṟṟuc cūḻalum aminta illam nakar eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar ciṟṟūriṉum virintatum pala ciṟṟūrkaḷit taṉṉakattuk koṇṭatumām paranta ūr nakarākiyatu. ikkāl ciṟṟūrkaḷum nakarkaḷākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. oru nakaruḷ pala nakarkaḷ irukka itukāl kāṇalām.
nakarāṭci, mānakarāṭci eṉpavi makkaḷ tokiyāl kaṇakkiṭu muṟi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam, uyirikaḷiṉ oruvaki nakarvaṉa eṉṟākiyamiyē.
nakar nakaram eṉṟum, nakari eṉṟum vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. nakarakam eṉpatu akanakar eṉal ākum. ṭavuṇcip (Township) eṉpatu nakaramākak kaliccollāyatu. ūrvalam pōl nakarvalamum ēṟpaṭṭatu.
nakarvalak kātal 
ulāk kāṭciyiṉāl uṇṭākiya kāmam mikuntāl ataṉip piṟaroṭum urittu varuntuvatu pavaṉik kātal eṉṉum peyar peṟum.
kāmaru mulāvaṟ kāṭci yālē
aṭinta kāma mikkā lavaṟṟip
piṟaroṭum eṭuttup pēci varuntutal
pavaṉik kātalām pakaruṅ kālē	- muttuvī. 1121
nakal 
naku + al = cirittal, makiḻtal.
palloḷi veḷippaṭal nakal, nakutal; naki, nakittal.
nakalvallar allārkku māyiru ñālam
pakalumpāl paṭṭaṉṟu iruḷ	- tiruk. 999
tavalarun tolkēḷvi taṉmi yuṭiyār
ikalilar eḵkuṭiyār tammuṭ kuḻīi
nakaliṉ iṉitāyiṟ kāṇpām akalvāṉattu
umpar uṟivār pati	- nālaṭi. 137
nakulam 
melliyatum miṉuṅkuvatumām muṭiyuṭiyatumām kīri nakulam eṉappaṭum.
māntarkku nalam ceytal koṇṭē nakulam (nalla piṟappu) eṉṟum, kīrip piḷḷi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatām. piḷḷi nakulam eṉpataṟkuk kīrikkuṭṭi eṉpār arumpata uriyāciriyar (cilap. 15:54). kuṭṭi eṉṉum iḷamip peyar kīrikkum uritteṉpar mayilinātar (naṉ. 387 - u.vē.cā. aṭik.).
naki 
nakutal  > naki.
naki = oḷiyuṭiya pal. nakutal = makiḻtal.
nakum poḻutu pal veḷippaṭṭu oḷi ceytalāl naki eṉappaṭṭatu.
nakutaṟ poruṭṭaṉṟu naṭṭal	- tiruk. 784
eṉpatu atu.
nakiyil, eḷḷal nakiyum uṇṭu. atu,
paṉṉakamum nakuveḷḷip paṉivari	- kampa. pāla. 324
eṉpatu.
kātal veḷippāṭum nakiyākkum eṉpatu,
nakunayam maṟittal
eṉṉum tolkāppiyattāl (1207) aṟiyalām.
poṉṉakiyiṟ ciṟantatu puṉṉaki
eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
naki naṭṭu 
naki= iḻi eṉappaṭum. taṇṭaṭṭi, pāmpaṭam ,pōlvaṉa.
naṭṭu= muruku, koppu, kāppu, tōṭu mutaliyaṉa.
muṉṉatu pēraṇikalaṅkaḷiyum, piṉṉatu ciṟṟaṇi kalaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkum. muṉṉavaṟṟilum tiruku amippu iruntālum, piṉṉavaṟṟil tirukamippē ciṟappum taṉit taṉmiyum uṭiyatātal karutuka. muṉṉavaṟṟil paḷicciṭalum acivum piṉṉavaṟṟil acivilā nilippum uṇmi karutuka. peṇṇukku naki naṭṭu eṉṉa pōṭṭīrkaḷ eṉṟu viṉavār ikkālattu ariyar.
nakippatam 
naki = oḷi, makiḻvu, puṉmuṟuval. patam = pakkuvam.
vārittaraḷa nakiceytu vaṉcem pavaḷa vāymalarntu	- cilap. 7:38
tavattō rāyiṉum takimalar vaṇṭiṉ
nakippatam pārkkum iḷiyō rāyiṉum	- cilap. 14:162-163
nakiyāyam 
nakiyamar āyam	- pu.ve. 75
iṉṉaki yāyam	- ciṟupāṇ. 220
poruḷ: iyalici nāṭakattālum iṉiya moḻikaḷālum iṉiya makiḻcciyic ceyyum tiraḷ (nac. uri).
nakivēḷampar 
  naki= makiḻvu, nakiccuvi.
vēḷ= mūṅkil; vēy  > vēḷ.
ampar= uyaram; uyarviṭam.
kaḻimēl ēṟi makkaḷ makiḻvuṟak kūttuc ceyyum kaḻikkūttar, nakivēḷampar eṉappaṭuvār. vaḷikka vaḷiyum vēy maṉṉar muṭi mēlām; vaḷikka vaḷiyāta vēy vēḷampar kippukuntu mētiṉi ellām uḻalum 				       taṉip.
nakkattira māli (uṭumāli) 
nakutal = oḷi ceytal; nakkattiram = oḷiyuṭiyatu.
uṭuviṉ peyar ovvoṉṟum varumāṟu iṉiya pāṭal irupattēḻu iyaṟṟit toṭuttal nakkattiramāli eṉappaṭum. uṭuviṉ eṇṇikki 27 ākaliṉ, innūṟ pāṭal tokiyum 27 āyiṟṟu.
uṭuppērk kārttiruttal cantamēṟṟa naṭcatra māliyē- pira. tira. 26

* tāraka māli kāṇka.
nakkal 
nakutal, nakittal, naki, eṉpaṉa eḷḷutal poruḷil varuvaṉa.
eḷḷal iḷami pētimi maṭaṉeṉ
ṟuḷḷap paṭṭa naki nāṉkeṉpa
eṉṉum tolkāppiya (1198) muṉvippu eḷḷal eṉpatē.. kēli ceytal eṉṉum poruḷil viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭārattil nakkal eṉpatu vaḻaṅkappaṭiṉum potu vaḻakkeṉa virivuṟṟatu atu. nakicci eṉṉum nakkal poruḷtarum collum viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
eṉṉa nakkalā ceykiṟāy?	 ma.va.
nakkal narakal 
nakkal= nakiyāṭum col.
narakal= aruvaṟuppāṉa col.
nakkal narakal pēcci nammiṭam vittuk koḷḷātē eṉṟu takavaṟṟa coṟkaḷik kaṭivar. nakutal = nakittal; nakkal eṉpatum atu. vēṭikki viḷiyāṭṭu, kēli kiṇṭal eṉṟu colpavi nakkalām.
iṭakkaraṭakku mutalām iḻinta coṟkaḷic collutal narakalām. narakal eṉpatu colliṉ taṉmi nōkkiya uvamic col.
nakkavā tukkavā (tuykkavā) 
nakkal   = nakki uṇṇal.
tukkal = nukartal, tuyttal.
viḻak kūṭāta iṭattil viḻunta tēṉi nakkavā tukkavā? eṉpar. iraṇṭaṟkum ākātu eṉpatām.
karumiyiṉiṭam akappaṭṭa poruḷ evarukkum utavātu pōvati viḷakka ivviṇiccol payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatām. avaṉ cottu nakkavā tukkavā ākum? eṉpar. tukkal = tuyttal; tuttal eṉpatum tuṟṟi eṉpatum atu.
nakkik kuṭittal 
nakkik kuṭittal = uḻiyāmal uṇṇal.
āṟṟil veḷḷam pōṉālum nāy nakkittāṉē kuṭikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. nāy nīri nakkik kuṭikkum. avvāṟē kañci cōṟu āyavaṟṟi nāy uṇpatum nakkutal eṉavē collappaṭum. nakkutal eṉpatu iḻivup poruḷ taruvatāka amintuviṭṭatu. atu pōl uḻiyāmalō, uḻippuk kiṭṭāmalō irantu uṇpati nakkik kuṭittal eṉpatu vaḻakkāka ūṉṟiviṭṭatu. nakutal eṉpatum taṉ uyarporuḷi viṭuttu iḻiporuḷukku iṭamākiyatum uṇṭu. nakiyāṇṭi eṉpatē niyāṇṭiyātal aṟika. niyāṇṭi mēḷam nāṭaṟi ceyti.
nakkutal 
tēṉ, kuḻampu, pāku mutaliyavaṟṟi nāvāl toṭṭuc cuvittu uṭkoḷvatu nakkutalām. itu nālvakiyāl uṇṇutal eṉpatil oru vakiyām. maṟṟavi uṇṇal, parukal, kaṭittal eṉpaṉavām. nakku nāyiṉum kaṭiyeṉap pukalum nāṉmaṟiyē eṉpatu nāvukkaracar tēvāram.
naṅku 
naṅku eṉpatu poṟāmi eṉṉum poruḷil kumari māvaṭṭa iraṇiyal vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. nallatu eṉṉum poruḷaminta naṉku eṉpatu naṅku ākiyirukkalām. nallatu illātati nallatu eṉpatu maṅkala vaḻakkākak koḷḷak kūṭiyatu. vēṟu vakiyākap poruḷ koḷḷavum iṭantarum col itu.
naṅkūram 
kappali nili niṟuttap payaṉpaṭum kūrnuṉi irumpuk kokkiyuṭiyatum kalappi vaṭivuṭiyatum naṅkūramām. oṉṟik kīḻē vikkum pōtu metuvāka vikkāmal valuvāka vittāl eṉṉa naṅkeṉa vikkiṟāy eṉṉum vaḻakku naṅkūram pāyccutali viḷakkum.
kūram = kūrmi aminta karuvi.
naṅki 
peṇṭiruḷ nallāḷ eṉṉum poruḷaminta naṅki eṉpatu nāttuṇiyāḷ eṉṉum uṟavumuṟic collākap paḻaṉi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkil uḷḷatu. nāttūṇ naṅki eṉṟu cilappatikārattu varum maturi āyarkuṭi vaḻakku, iṉṟum āviṉaṉkuṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka iruttal paḻamporuṭ putiyalāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatu. kaṇavariṉ mūttāḷi (akkiyi) naṅki eṉpatu kōvi vaḻakku.
naci 
naci = viruppam, āci. naccutal = virumputal.
nacitara vantōr nacipiṟak koḻiya
vacipaṭa vāḻntōr palarkol	- puṟam. 15
poruḷ: tam āci koṭuvara vantōr avvāci piṉṉoḻiya vaciyuṇṭāka uyir vāḻntōr palarō? (pa.u.).
naccāṭi 
naccu + āṭi = naccāṭi.
naccāṭi, naccāṭi tavirtta liṅkam (civaṉ) naccāṭaliṅkam eṉpavi cēṟṟūr vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. āṇ peṇ peyarkaḷum avvūril uṇṭu. avvūrt teyvap peyar atu.
naccu 
nañcu  > naccu.
nañcuṭiya pāmpu naccuppāmpu eṉappaṭal ma.va. naccuyiri eṉpatum makkaḷ vaḻakkē.
naccappaṭu poruḷiyum kuṟikkum.
poruḷuriyāḷar,
naccuk koṉṟēṟku naṉṉeṟi yuṇṭō?	- cilap. 16:65-66
poruḷ: meyyurikkum cāṉṟōrāl naccappaṭum toḻilik keṭuttēṉukku (a.pa. uri).
poruḷ: maṟaviyiṟ porunti yamintōr virumpappaṭum poruḷākiya nalla oḻukkattik keṭutta eṉakku iṉit tīkkatiyaṉṟi naṟkatiyuṇṭāmō? (aṭiyārk. uri).
uyirik keṭukkum naccukku viruppam eṉṉum poruḷtaral māṟaṉṟō eṉiṉ aṉṟām. avar ulaka vāḻvi veṟuttu iṟappē iṉitu eṉak koṇṭār ākaliṉ apporuḷ uṇṭāyiṟṟām.
nañcuk kuppiyē ñālum tāliyāyk koṇṭa vīṟuṭiyārkku iṟappu iṉpamē allavō!
cātaliṉ iṉṉāta tilli iṉitatūum
ītal iyiyāk kaṭi
eṉṉum vaḷḷuvam (tiruk. 230) nōkkuka.
pulavaṉukkup poruḷtara iyalavilliyē eṉat tali taruvataṟku vāḷtanta kumaṇaṉiyum, pulavaṉ vaṟumi tīrkka vāykkavilliyē eṉap pāmpup puṟṟil kiviṭṭa puravalaṉ oruvaṉiyum eṇṇuka.
naccup piccu 
naccup piccu:1
	naccu	= 	cottu muḻuvatiyum nittuviṭac ceykiṉṟa peruñcelavukaḷ.
	piccu	= 	cottip pirittup pirittu viṟkat takka ciṟu celavukaḷ.
naccu = nintu pōkac ceyvatu; piccu = piyntu pōkac ceyvatu. piyttuk koṭuttalip piccuk koṭuttal eṉpatum vaḻakkāṟu. piyttalip puyttal eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakkāṟu.
iṉi, naccup piccu eṉap pēcātē eṉpatum vaḻakkāṟu. naccarittum pittup piṭittum pēcutalic cuṭṭuvatu atu.
naccup piccu:2
	naccu	= 	nañcu pōl aḻikkum tīyar.
	piccu	= 	paṅkup pōṭṭu pirikkum uṭaṉ piṟantār.
naccup piccu etuvum illi; ātalāl kavali etuvum avarukku illi eṉpatu nayappuri. naccu, cottu muḻuvatiyum aḻittu viṭakkūṭiya tīya makkaḷ. piccu, paṅku pākam eṉṟu pirittuk koṇṭu pōkak kūṭiya uṭaṉpiṟantār. taṉi makaṉāka iruntu, avaṉukkuc cotti aḻikkum tīya makaṉum illāmal naṉmakaṉ iruntāl avaṉi naccup piccu illātavaṉ eṉpar.
nañcu 
nintu = nañcu.
uyiri nintu pōkac ceyyum - oḻintu pōkac ceyyum - poruḷ. ataṉ koṭumiyaṟintu naccu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
nañcuṭimi tāṉaṟintu nākam karantuṟiyum	- auvi. taṉip.
nañcuk koṭi 
kaṉṟukkum tāykkum toṭarpāka iruntatu nañcuk koṭi. kaṉṟu piṟanta pōtu atuvum veḷiyē taḷḷum. kaṉṟit taḷḷiyavuṭaṉ veḷippaṭāmal karuppiyil oṭṭik koṇṭiruntu paṭippaṭiyē viḻutalum uṇṭu. ap poḻutil nāy paṟṟāmal viḻippōṭu pārttiruttal vēṇṭum. nañcuk koṭi iḷaṅkoṭi eṉavum paṭum. pāl maraṅkaḷil ataṉik kaṭṭum paḻakkam mūṭattaṉattāl ēṟpaṭṭatu maṭṭumaṉṟu pāl maram potumaramām. ātalāl potumik kēṭumām.
pālmarattil nañcuk koṭiyik kaṭṭiṉāl pāl curakkum eṉṉum paḻakkam kēṭāṉatām.
naṭakki 
naṭattalākiya ceyaliyum naṭattiyākiya paṇpiyum terivikkum col. oruvar uṭaliyakkamum uḷaviyakkamum oruṅkē kāṭṭum ipporuḷ naṭatti eṉpataṟkum uriyatām. naṭi eṉpatum ām.
ñālattu varūum naṭakki	- tol. 1057
ēṟupōl pīṭu naṭi	- tiruk. 59
naṉṉaṭattic cāṉṟitaḻ	- vaḻakku.
naṭappu 
naṭappu = naṭakkum ceyti, āṇṭu.
ippoḻutu ceyya muṭiyātu; naṭappukkup pārkkalām eṉpatu vaḻakku. naṭappu eṉpatu etirvarum āṇṭu eṉpatām. itil naṭakkum āṇṭi naṭappu eṉpatu vaḻakkilli. āṉāl, nāṭṭu naṭappu eppaṭi irukkiṟatu eṉpatil naṭappu eṉpatu naṭimuṟi eṉṟum nikaḻcci eṉṟum poruḷ taruvatāy amikiṉṟatu.
avaṉ naṭappuc cariyilli eṉpatil naṭappu oḻukkap poruḷ tarutal veḷippaṭi. āyiṉum, naṭappu, naṭi eṉpataṉ vaḻiyākavē varukiṉṟatu.
naṭam 
naṭṭam  > naṭam = kūttu.
naṭṭuvakkāl kūttukkali.
tāṇṭavam eṉpatu iyalpāṉa kūttiṉum mēmpaṭṭa tiṟattatu. oṟṟikkāl ūṉṟi oṟṟikkāl eṭuttu talimēlvara uyarttiyum kalipala kāṭṭi āṭal.
tāṇṭavam puriyavalla tampirāṉ	- periyapu.
eṉṟu naṭavaracaṉum,
eṭutta poṟpātam
eṉṟu avaṉ tiruvaṭiyum kūṟappaṭṭaṉavām.
naṭali 
nallateṉak koḷḷattakka uṟutippāṭu etuvum illātavar; pōlimiyar; vañcar.
nañciṟ koṭiyāṉ naṭalit toḻilāl
tuñcuṟ ṟaṉaṉō	- kampa. āraṇ. 1102
naṭavu 
nelli vitittu nāṟṟaṅkālil vaḷarac ceytu uriya paruvattil nāṟṟip paṟittut toḷinilattil ūṉṟutal naṭavu eṉappaṭum. naṭutal, naṭuki eṉpavum itu.
naṭṭavā meṉa vīḻntaṉa	- kampa. uyut. 1437
naṭavi 
	naṭavi 	= 	naṭattaṟkuriya vaḻi.
kāṉyāṟṟu naṭavi	- malipaṭu. 214
poruḷ: kāṭṭāṟṟu vaḻi 		(nac. uri).
naṭu 
iṭiveḷippaṭa ūṉṟutal naṭutal. naṭavu naṭuki eṉpatum atu. payirkaḷi naṭutal, tūṇkaḷ naṭutal, maram naṭutal aṟika. naṭuvē niṟkum camaṉkōl pōlavum, ucciyil niṟkum katir pōlavum cāyātu ūṭu niṟṟal naṭuvunilimi.
camaṉceytu cīrtūkkum kōlpōl amintorupāl
kōṭāmi cāṉṟōrk kaṇi	- tiruk. 118
naṭukal 
amariṭi iṟantāṉ amaraṉ. avaṉ peyarum pīṭum kallil eḻuti māli cūṭṭi, pīli cātti, paṭiyaliṭṭu vaḻipaṭal vaḻakkāyiṟṟu.
kaṟpuṭip peṇṇām kaṇṇakikku naṭukal aṉṟik kōyilum eṭuttami cilappatikāram. pīṭum peyarum eḻutiya piṟaṅkunili naṭukal paṟṟic caṅkanūlkaḷ palapaṭak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. naṭukaṟkaḷ itukāṟum āṅkāṅkuk kāṇavum paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭci kālkōḷ nīrppaṭi naṭukal
perumpaṭi vāḻttal
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1006).
naṭukal vaki 
ūrkāttāṉ naṭukal.
peṇ mīṭṭāṉ naṭukal.
aṟam kāttāṉ naṭukal.
pulikutti naṭukal.
paṉṟi kutti naṭukal.
kutiri kutti naṭukal.
āvaṭṭi (ā kavarum veṭcip pōril iṟantāṉ naṭukal.)
tīppāynta ammi naṭukal
naṭukkam 
niliyāka nillāmal atirntu vitirvitirttu naṭuṅkutal.
atirvum vitirvum naṭukkam ceyyum	- tol. 799
akavi mutirvālum accattālum uṇṭākum meyppāṭu naṭukkam.
aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟiyeṉap
piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē	- tol. 1202
naṭukkūṟu 
naṭuppakuti, naṭuppākam eṉṉum poruḷaminta naṭukkūṟu eṉṉum collukku, naḷḷiravu eṉṉum poruḷ ucilampaṭṭi vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. naṭuppakal, naṇpakal. naṭu iravu, naḷḷiravu. pakal iravu ākiya iraṇṭaṉ naṭuvukkum potuvākiyatu. iravu maṭṭum kuṟippatu av vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka irukkiṉṟatu. kūṟu = pakuti.
naṭucceṅkali uruval 
naṭucceṅkali uruval = oru tīmiyāl pala tīmikku āḷākkal.
oru taḷattiṉ naṭuvēyuḷḷa ceṅkali uruviṉāl ataṉ pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ceṅkalkaḷum ovvoṉṟākac carintu taḷamē pāḻpaṭa nērum. avvāṟē ōr ariya ceyalil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa pōtu ataṟku accāṇiyāka irukkum oru ceyalit taṭippaṭutti viṭuvatu muḻuttaṭiyum ceytatākavē muṭiyum. aṉṟiyum muḻuppayaṉ iḻappuṭaṉ vīṇ muyaṟciyum, celavum, maṉattuyarum uṇṭākavē iṭamām. naṉṟākat toḻil ōṭik koṇṭirunta pōtu naṭucceṅkali uruvuvatu pōla uruviviṭṭāṉē eṉpatu vaḻakkuri.
naṭunāḷ 
nāḷ naṭu  > naṭunāḷ.
naṭunāḷ vantu tumpiyum tuvikkum	- puṟam. 280
naṭunāḷ yāmattum pakalum tuñcāṉ	- puṟam. 189
poruḷ: iṭi yāmattum naṇpakalum tuyilāṉāy 								      (pa.u.).
naṭuvaṇ 
naṭuvu + aṇ = naṭuvaṇ = naṭuvāka amital.
vaṭuvilā viyattu maṉṉiya mūṉṟil
naṭuvaṇatu eyta irutaliyum eytum
naṭuvaṇatu eytātāṉ eytum ulippey
taṭuvatu pōlum tuyar	- nālaṭi. 114
naṭuvaṇ aracu eṉpatu iṟṟikkāla vaḻakkuttoṭar.
naṭuvunili 
orupāl cāyātu camaṉkōl pōl oppa niṟkum nili.
camaṉceytu cīrtūkkum kōlpōl amintorupāl
kōṭāmi cāṉṟōrk kaṇi	- tiruk. 118
atikārap peyar naṭuvunilimi. naṭunili eṉṉum col, camaṉceytu cīrtūkkum tulikkōliṉ naṭuniṉṟu orupāṟ kōṭāta nāviṉ niliyiṉiṉṟu eḻuntatu.
takuti paṟṟi oruvari oruvar oruviṉikku amarttum pōtum, tiṟami paṟṟi oru tuṟiyiṟ ciṟantavarkkup paricaḷikkum pōtum, vilikkuk koḷḷum poruṭṭup paṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ nallaṉavaṟṟit terintu eṭukkum pōtum, kuṟṟac cāṭṭup paṟṟi oruvar naṭattiyi āyntu tīrppuk kūṟum pōtum paki naṭpu notumal (ayal) eṉṉum muttiṟattum ottiruntu uṇmippaṭi oḻukutal. naṉṟi ceytavariṭattum kaṇṇōṭi naṭunili tiṟampak kūṭāteṉpataṟku itu ceynnaṉṟi yaṟitaliṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. (tiruk. marapuri pak. 92).
naṭuvoliyantāti:
* oliyantāti kāṇka.
naṭi 
	naṭi 	= 	oḻukkam, vaḻi, vāyil, paḻakkavaḻakkam, celvam, muṟimi.
naṭi keṭṭavaṉ eṉpatu oḻukkam keṭṭavaṉ eṉṉum 								        ma.va.
vaṭṭam cuṟṟi vaḻiyē pō eṉpatu paḻamoḻi yāṉālum, kuṟukkē curukkamākap pōkum naṭivaḻi oṉṟi makkaḷ uṇṭākki viṭutal naṭimuṟi. naṭivaḻiyil uṭkārātē eṉpatu vāyililum, vāyil nērilum uṭkārātē eṉpatām. naṭiyaṟiyātavaṉ ayalūrāṉ; pōkaṭṭum viṭuṅkaḷ eṉpatil paḻakka vaḻakkam muṟimip poruḷkaḷ uḷa.
naṉṉaṭi nalkal vēntarkkuk kaṭaṉē	- puṟam. 312
eṉpatil paricu pārāṭṭu virutu koṭi eṉpavi uḷa.

naṭiyaṉ 
naṭappavaṉ naṭiyaṉ eṉappaṭal potuvaḻakku. naṭakka utavum mitiyaṭiyi naṭiyaṉ eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku. ōriṭattu niṉṟu mēyātu pacci kāṇum pakkamellām aliyum āṭṭi naṭiyaṉ eṉpatu kuṟṟāla vaḻakku.
naṭṭaṇi 
colvatik kēḷāmal vīmpu ceytalum tāṉ colliyatic cātittalum naṭṭaṇi eṉappaṭum. nimirntu niṟkum aṇipōl āṭṭa aciviṉṟi niṟkum niliyic cuṭṭiyatu atu. naṭṭaṇikkāraṉ naṭṭaṇi ceyyātē eṉpavi teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku. naṭṭaṇikkāl eṉpatu kālmēl kāl pōṭṭiruttal. piṟari matiyātiruttalumām.
naṭṭaṇikkāl 
naṭṭaṇam, naṭṭaṇikaḷukkuk kūttu, kōmāḷik kūttu, kaṇavaṉ maṉivi pōṉṟavaruḷ oṟṟumiyiṉmi, naṭippu koṭumi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum, naṭṭaṇikkāraṉ eṉpataṟkuk karuvaṅ koṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷum tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (ce.ta.co.pi. akaramutali)
naṭṭu + aṇi + kāl = naṭṭaṇikkāl.
naṭṭaṇikkāl eṉpatu oṟṟik kāli nilattil ūṉṟi maṟṟik kālik kuṟukkāka akkāliṉ mēl ūṉṟi iruttal. cuvaril cāyntu koṇṭum naṭṭaṇikkāliṉ niṟṟal uṇṭu.
kokku oru kāli ūṉṟi oru kāli eṭuttu maṭakki niṟṟali ōrāṟṟāṉ naṭṭaṇik kālukku oppiṭalām. eṭutta kāli aṭutta kāloṭu porutti nillāmi oṉṟē kokkiṉ oṟṟik kāl nilikkum naṭṭaṇikkāl nilikkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭām.
oṟṟik kālil niṟṟal eṉpatu keṭupiṭiyāka niṉṟu, niṉittati niṟivēṟṟik koḷḷac ceyyum naṭavaṭik kiyākak karutappaṭum. koṭuttē tīra vēṇṭum eṉṟu oṟṟik kālil niṉṟu vāṅkiviṭṭāṉ eṉpatu vaḻaṅkumuṟi. itil iruntu naṭṭaṇi eṉpataṟkuk keṭupiṭiyāka niṟivēṟṟal, muṟikēṭu vaṉpukku niṟṟal eṉap poruḷkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
naṭṭaṇi ceyyātē, coṉṉāl kēḷ eṉṉum kaṭṭaḷiyil iruntē naṭṭaṇi, collukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭātatu eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatāyiṟṟē.
kālmēl kāl pōṭutal avikkup poruntā nikaḻcci eṉappaṭuvatuṭaṉ cerukkukku aṭiyāḷamākavum nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷāl iṉṟum karutappaṭukiṟatu. vaḷḷalār añciyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kālmēl kāl vittiruttal.
kāṭṭuyar aṇimēl irukkavum payantēṉ;
			kāliṉmēl kālvikkap payantēṉ;
pāṭṭayal kēṭkap pāṭavum payantēṉ;
			pañcaṇi paṭukkavum payantēṉ;
nāṭṭiya uyarnta tiṇṇimēl iruntu
			naṉkuṟak kaḷittukkāl kīḻē
nīṭṭavum payantēṉ; nīṭṭip pēcutali
	niṉikkavum payantaṉaṉ entāy!
vaḷḷalār pāṭiya piḷḷic ciṟuviṇṇappattuḷ oṉṟu itu.
naṭṭam 
naṭṭam:1
acivaṟac ceytal naṭutal eṉappaṭum. naṭṭa kallum pēcumō? eṉpatu civavākkiyar pāṭal. naṭṭukku niṟuttal eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
naṭṭam eṉpatu nili iḻantu poruḷ iḻantu ceyalaṟa niṟkum niliyik kuṟittatu. ataṉik kaṭṭam eṉpatik kaṣṭam ākkiyatu pōl, naṣṭam ākkiviṭṭaṉar.
naṭṭam:2
naṭṭam = naṭaṉam, āṭal.
naḷḷiravil naṭṭam payiṉṟāṉi	 civapurāṇam.
naṭṭu āṭal - kāli ūṉṟi - patittu - āṭal naṭam.
tāḷippatittal = patam.
naṭṭāṟṟil viṭutal 
naṭṭāṟṟil viṭutal = oru paṇiyiṉ naṭuvē kiviṭutal.
naṭṭāṟu (naṭu + āṟu) vari veḷḷattil paṭakil ēṟṟik koṇṭu pōy iṭiyē uṉpāṭu eṉa veḷḷattil taḷḷiviṭṭāl avaṉ pāṭu eṉṉām? kariyilēyē iruntirup piṉ, veḷḷam vaṭintapiṉ vantiruppāṉ. nīntik kaṭakka muṭiyumā eṉa etirnōkki yiruppāṉ. ivaṟṟukku vāyppetuvum illāmal veḷḷattōṭu veḷḷamākap pōka viṭutal veṅkoṭumiyām. ippaṭi ōr iṭarāṉa ceyalit toṭaṅki, ataṉ iṭiyē utavāmalum etiriṭṭum iruttal naṭṭāṟṟil viṭutalām. itu taḷḷal, kiviṭal eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
naṭṭukku naṭu 
naṭṭu= perumparappil naṭuvāka aminta iṭam.
naṭu= naṭuvē yaminta iṭattiṉ cariyāṉa miyappuḷḷi.
naṭuppakuti vēṟu; naṭuppakutiyiṉ miyam vēṟu. vaṭṭattuḷ vaṭṭam miyam eṉiṉum ataṉ miyappuḷḷi naṭṭa naṭuvākum naṭṭukku naṭu, naṭuvukku naṭu, naṭṭa naṭu eṉpaṉavellām oru poruḷaṉavām. naṭṭāṟu eṉpatu naṭuvāṟu eṉṟālum naṭṭa naṭu āṟu eṉpatē cariyāṉa naṭuvām. naṭṭa naṭuvil oru puḷḷi vi eṉpataṟkum. naṭuvil puḷḷi vi eṉpataṟkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
naṭpiyal 
naṇpu  > naṭpu + iyal = naṭpiyal.
uṭiyaḻi kāli utaviya kipōl
naṭali tīrttal naṇpaṉa tiyalpu	- peruṅ. 5:3:39-40
naṭpu 
naṭpu eṉṉum collit tiruvaḷḷuvar peruvaḻakkāka āḷkiṟār. kēṇmi, toṭarpu, paḻakkam, oṉṟutal eṉpavaṟṟiyum vaḻaṅkukiṟār. paḻimi eṉpatu vaḻivaḻiyāka varum naṭpākum.
naṭpu, kātal poruḷil varutal,
uṭampo ṭuyiriṭi eṉṉamaṟ ṟaṉṉa
maṭantiyo ṭemmiṭi naṭpu	- tiruk. 1122
eṉpatu naṭpu kātal poruḷatākum.
naṭpu eṉpatu naḷ eṉṉum vēr vaḻiyāka vanta col. 
naḷ + pu = naṭpu; naḷḷutal = neruṅkutal; ceṟital.
naṭpum kātalum 
naṭpu eṉpatu uḷḷattu aḷavāl kātalukku oppāṉatu. ak kātal aḷavil nillāmal iṭittuk kūṟum āciriyaṉ pōlavum, uriya poḻutil taṉṉiyum koṭukkum koṭiyāḷaṉ pōlavum amivatu atu.
kātalām tuṇi oṉṟē. āṉāl, naṭpām tuṇi avvoṉṟu eṉṉum aḷavil niṟpatu illi. caṅkappalaki pōlat takkārk kellām takutiyāṉa iṭantantu viriyum viriviṉatu atu.
kātal, pāl vēṟupāṭu uṭiyatu. āṉāl naṭpu, avvēṟupāṭum illātatu.
kātal tuṇi, vīṭṭaḷavil kuṭumpa aḷavil niṟṟal perumpālum vaḻakkamāka uḷḷatu. naṭput tuṇi iṭaṅkaṭanta viriviṉatu.
irumaṉam kūṭiṉāl tirumaṇam ākum. avvāṟē irumaṉam ōrotta iyivē iṉiya naṭpum ākum.
kātal veṟṟi ettakiya iṉpa nalamum, vāḻvuc ciṟappum tarumō, avvaḷavu iṉpa nalamum vāḻvuc ciṟappum nalla naṭpum tarum.
kātal tōlvi ettakiya tuṉpamum vāḻvukkēṭum tarumō, avvaḷavu tuṉpamum vāḻvuk kēṭum tīya naṭpum tarum.
puṇarcci paḻakā uṇarcci mēmpāṭṭup picirāntiyār kōpperuñcōḻaṉār naṭpu, nāṭu kaṭantu ōṭi varavum uyirōṭu oṉṟavum ceytatu varalāṟu.
cīṉakkar poyyā moḻiyār naṭpu, paṭukkiyil cellakkiṭa eṉṟataṟkāka iṟuti neruppup paṭukkiyil kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyoṭu pulavar poyyāmoḻiyārum kūṭiya mūvar cellakkiṭa eṉṉum paṭukki āyatu. cellakkiṭa = cellāmal paṭuttuk kiṭa.
pāriyiṉ naṭpuk kapilar, pāriyiṉ makaḷirkkut tantiyākic ceykaṭaṉ ceytu muṭittu mēlum vāḻa virumpāmal peṇṇiyāṟṟaṅ kariyil centaḻal vaḷarttu atil pukum vīṭupēṟṟiṉpatti ūṭṭiyatu.
kaṉalpuku kapilak kal atu
eṉak kalleḻuttum āyatu.
naṭpum pakiyum 
naṭpu paki eṉpavi etiriṭikaḷ. naḷ eṉpataṉ aṭiyāka naṭpum, pakku paku eṉpataṉ aṭiyākap pakiyum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉatu iṇital karuttilum piṉṉatu pirital karuttilum varuvaṉa.
paki eṉpataṟkut tamiḻil mikappala coṟkaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. aṉittiyum tiraṭṭavilli eṉiṉum, aritiṉ muyaṉṟu aimpatu coṟkaḷ aḷavu tiraṭṭik kāṭṭiya akara mutalikaḷ uḷa. āyiṉ iṉṉum avveṇṇikki yaḷavuḷḷa coṟkaḷ iṇikkat takkaṉavāka eñciyuḷḷaṉa.
paki ivvāṟāka naṭpu eṉpataṟkō av virinta akara mutalikaḷum kēṇmi, kēḷ, toṭarpu eṉac colli amintaṉa. iccuṭṭum collum naṇparkaḷ vāyttaliṉ curukkap pāṭṭiyum, pakivarkaḷ kiḷartaliṉ perukkap pāṭṭiyum collaḷavālum cuṭṭuvaṉavām.
tamiḻil amintuḷḷa etirc coṟkaḷ perumpālum aṭiyoṟṟi amivaṉa. ātalāl, pakiccol koṇṭu nakiccol allatu naṭpuc collik kaṇṭu koḷḷak kūṭuvatām.
naṭpiyalik karutiṉāl, neruṅkutal, poruntutal, kalattal, oṉṟutal, nilittal eṉṉum paṭimāṉa vaḷarcciyik kāṇa iyalum. neruṅkutalāl poruntutalum, poruntu talāl kalattalum, kalattalāl oṉṟutalum, oṉṟutalāl nilittalum nikaḻvaṉavām.
ivvaṇṇamē, pakiyiyalil neruṅkāmi, poruntāmi, kalavāmi, oṉṟāmi, niliyāmi eṉpavi ēṟpaṭutali aṟiyak kūṭum. neruṅkāmiyāl poruntāmiyum, porun tāmiyāl kalavāmiyum, kalavāmiyāl oṉṟāmiyum, oṉṟāmiyāl niliyāmiyum ēṟpaṭutal veḷippaṭiyē!
naṭpiyal pakiyiyal eṉpavaṟṟi ēraṇa muṟiyil allatu iyaṟkiyoṭu poruntiya aṟiviyal muṟiyil āyntu muntiyōr kuṟiyīṭu ceytuḷḷami koḷḷi makiḻvu taruvatākum. itaṉi aṟintu makiḻumāṟu naṭpup poruṭcoṟkaḷiyum pakipporuṭ coṟkaḷiyum paṭṭiyaliṭṭu ivaṇ cuṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉavām.
tamiḻil coṟpañcam illi! avaṟṟit tiraṭṭi oḻuṅkuṟuttip pōṟṟāp pañcamē vaḻivaḻi vāṭāmal vāyttuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu. carukkari āliyāḷaṉukkuc carukkari nōy vantatip pōlvatu itu!

pakimi, pakivar cuṭṭiya coṟkaḷukkellām perum pālum paḻañcol āṭciyuṇmi kaḻaka nūṟ payiṟciyāḷar kāṇat takkaṉavē. aritāka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iyikkap peṟṟuḷa. cāṉṟu - cēkkāḷi, cērttāḷi, pēcār, toṭuppar.

naṇṭu 
marutanilak karupporuḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu naṇṭu. atu palapeyarkaḷit tāṅkum.
alavaṉ naḷḷi kuḷirñeṇ ṭārmati
kaḷavaṉ eṉṟivi kaṟkaṭakap peyarē
eṉpatu tivākaram. aiṅkuṟunūṟṟil kaḷvaṉ pattu eṉpatu oṉṟu. atu ēṭu eḻutuvōr patippittōr āyavarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭa piḻiyākum. talivaṉ taliviyar cantitta iṭattil cāṉṟāka iruntavar evarum ilar. oru naṇṭu avarkaḷ kaṇkāṇat tōṉṟiyatu; atuvē kaḷa - iṭa-c cāṉṟāka iruntamiyāl kaḷavaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu.
puḷḷik kaḷavaṉ puṉalcēr potukkam
eṉpatu kalittoki (88).
naṇṭum tumpiyum nāṉkaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (1530).
meyyuṭimiyiṉ ūṟṟuṇarvum, irikōṭaliṉ nāvuṇarvum, nāṟṟam kōṭaliṉ mūkkuṇarvum, kaṇṇuṭimiyiṉ kaṇṇuṇarvu muṭiyavāyiṉa; naṇṭiṟku mūkkuṇṭō eṉiṉ, aḵtu āciriyaṉ kūṟalāṉ uṇṭu eṉpatu eṉṟār pērāciriyar (uri).
naṇṭu nīrvaḷamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil, maram ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ vēr aṭiyilum vayal varappukaḷilum aḷi vakuttuk koṇṭu vāḻum. itaṉi, taṇṇaka maṇṇaḷic cellum (27), taṇṇaka maṇṇaḷi niṟiya (30) eṉa aiṅkuṟunūṟu colkiṟatu.
naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷ uṭaliṉmēl uyarntu vēmpiṉ arumpupōl tōṉṟum aḻaki, vēppu naṉi aṉṉa neṭuṅkaṇ eṉkiṟatu (30). puḷḷikaḷ palavaṟṟi yuṭimiyāl, puḷḷik kaḷvaṉ eṉkiṟatu (21). naṇṭiṉ kālkaḷil kūrmiyāṉa arampam pōṉṟa nakaṅkaḷ iruttalāl payir ceṭi koṭikaḷi aṟukkum.
kaḷvaṉ āmpal aṟukkum (21)
kaḷvaṉ vaḷḷi meṉkāl aṟukkum (26)
vittiya veṇmuḷi kaḷvaṉ aṟukkum (29)
vayalic ceṅkoṭi kaḷvaṉ aṟukkum (25)
eṉak kūṟum.
tāyuyircāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉ
eṉkiṟatu aiṅkuṟunāṟu (24). piṟarum avvāṟē kūṟiṉar. āṉāl iṟantatākak kiṭanta niliyē yaṉṟi iṟantatu illiyām.
ciṉi (muṭṭi) payanta alavaṉ, uṭal curuṅkip piṉ muṉṉiyiṉum paruttal vēṇṭi ōṭumāṟṟat toṭaṅkum. ōṭu kaḻalum alavaṉ melintu puttōṭu peṟuṅkāl, ataṉ vālil oṭṭik kiṭanta ciṉikaḷ porittut tāyalavaṉi nīṅkum. ciṉi payavāmuṉ irunta nilimiyiṉ vēṟāyp puttōṭum paruvuṭalum peṟuvāṉ pularntu ōyntu oṭuṅkik kiṭakkum naṇṭu iṟantatu pōlat tōṉṟutaliṉ nam nāṭṭup paṇṭic cāṉṟōr
tāyuyir vēṇṭāk kūrukir alavaṉ
eṉṟum (tol. poruḷ. nac. 157 mēṟ),
tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉ
eṉṟum (aiṅ. 24) kūṟiṉar eṉṟu viḷakkukiṟār urivēntar auvi. cu. tu.
naṇṭu īra maṇṇil celluṅkāl ataṉ kālkaḷ patintu varikaḷākat tōṉṟutalāl,
taṇcēṟu kaḷvaṉ varikkum
eṉkiṟatu aiṅkuṟunūṟu (28). varital varital eḻututalām. ataṉvari, eḻuttākat tōṟṟam taruvatum uṇṭu. ātalāl,
nantu nāku nīr eḻuttāyt tōṉṟiṉum tōṉṟum eṉpar.
naṇṭu nacukku 
	naṇṭu	= 	ōṭi āṭit tiriyum vaḷarnta kuḻantikaḷ.
	nacukku	= 	ōṭi āṭāmal nakarntum kiṭantum tavaḻn tum irukkum kuḻantikaḷ.
naṇṭu eṉpati naṇṭu ciṇṭu eṉpatil kāṇka. nacukku ciṟitu eṉṉum poruḷatu. nacukaṇi eṉpatoru nōy. ataṉ pūcci pacipōl oṭṭik kiṭappatām. nacukkutal tēyttalukku uriya noymiyāṉatu eṉpatu teḷivu.
naṇṭum ciṇṭum 
	naṇṭu	= 	ōṭi āṭit tiriyum piḷḷikaḷ.
	ciṇṭu	= 	ōṭi āṭit tiriyāmal tavaḻntum ūrn tum tiriyum piḷḷikaḷ.
naṇṭum ciṇṭumākat tirikiṉṟaṉa naṇṭuñ ciṇṭumākap pala piḷḷikaḷ eṉpavi vaḻakku. ciṇṭu, cuṇṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. naṇṭu, ciṇṭu eṉpavi periya naṇṭu, naṇṭuk kuñcu eṉpavaṟṟic cuṭṭi, attaku kuḻantikaḷik kuṟippatāyiṟṟām.
ciṇṭu, cuṇṭu eṉpavi ciṟiyatu eṉṉum poruḷ tarum coṟkaḷ. ciṟu kuṭumiyic ciṇṭu eṉpatiyum, ciṟukāyic cuṇṭikkāy eṉpatiyum karutuka. cuṇṭik kāyic curikkāy ākkiviṭṭāṉē eṉpatum viḷakkum.
naṇṭūri nariyūri 
ciṟumiyarkaḷ viḷiyāṭum oruvaki viḷiyāṭṭu. orutti kaṇṇik kaṭṭiviṭṭu avaḷ ki tōḷ kāl mutaliyavaṟṟil orutti viralāl taṭavi naṇṭūri nariyūri eṉak kūṟi oḷintu viṭuvāḷ. avaḷit tēṭip piṭippatu anta viḷiyāṭṭu. cariyākak kaṇṭupiṭikkavilli eṉṟāl mīḷavum kaṇṇik kaṭṭi naṇṭūri nariyūri eṉṟu toṭarvatu av viḷiyāṭṭu.
naṇṇutal 
naḷ  > naṇ  > naṇṇutal.
naḷ = ceṟivu, nerukkam. naḷḷiravu, naṇpakal.
naṇṇum iruviṉiyum naṇṇumiṉkaḷ nallaṟamē- cilap. 16 veṇ.

naṇpu 
naṭpu  > naṇpu.
naḷ  > naṇ = nerukkam, ceṟivu.
ceṟinta toṭarpukku naṭpu, naṇpu eṉpavi peyarāyiṉa.
kaṇpōl naṇpiṟ kēḷir	- puṟam. 71
naṇṇār, naṇṇātār = neruṅkār, pakivar.
naṇṇār naṭukkuṟa	- maṇimē. 22:29
nattam 
eppoḻutum āṭu māṭu makkaḷ paṟavi eṉa oli ōyātu viḷaṅkum iṭam nattam ākum. ūrūr tōṟum nattam uṇṭu. atu taṉiyoruvar iṭamaṉṟu. ūrp potuvukkeṉa viṭappaṭṭa aracu iṭam.
nattam pāḻpaṭṭāl ūrē pāḻpaṭṭatākap poruḷām. ūrp peyarkaḷē nattam eṉal uṇṭu. nattam, ālaṭi nattam, āval nattam, cāval nattam.
nattam puṟampōkku 
	nattam		= 	ūrkkup potuvām manti.
	puṟampōkku	= 	āṭu māṭu mēytaṟkeṉa aracu 		otukkiya pulnilam.
ūr māṭu, āṭu mutaliyavi nattattil taṅkum. nāykaḷum nattattil tiriyum. ‘nāy kurittu nattam pāḻākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
puṟampōkku mēyccal nilam eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum.
nattam ūraṭi cārntatu; puṟampōkku ūr nilapulaṅkaḷiyellām tāṇṭiya āṟu, kuḷam, ōṭi mutaliyavi.
nattal naṟuṅkal 
	nattal		= 	tiṉṉutaṟku vāy alanta kuḻanti.
	naṟuṅkal		= 	cavalip piḷḷi (a) nōyāl naṟuṅkip pōṉa		 piḷḷi.
nattutal = ārvappaṭutal; naṟuṅkutal = vaḷarcci iṉṟi iruttal. ittaku kuḻantikaḷ peṟṟōrkku ōyāta tolli tantu koṇṭē irukkum. aḻukiyum araṟṟumāka irukkum. nattal naṟuṅkali vittuk koṇṭu eppaṭi ellāvaṟṟiyum nēram kālattil muṭikka muṭiyum eṉa ēṅkuvār palar.
nattutal 
natti ōyvu oḻivu illāmal maṇṇi uṇṭu koḷḷuva koṇṭu taḷḷuva taḷḷum iyalpiṉatu pōl ōyātu virumpi uṇpatum vāyttati ellām tuyppatumāka iruttali nattutal eṉpatu teṉtamiḻaka vaḻakku. nattuvāy eṉpār pāratiyār.
nattimaṇ 
natti vāḻum cēṟṟumaṇ, kaḷimaṇṇākavum keṭṭit taṉmiyatākavum irukkum. am maṇṇik koṇṭu cuvar vittālum, mukaṭu parappiṉālum nīrāl kariyāta keṭṭit taṉmiyatu. ātalāl, nattimaṇ eṉappaṭum karuñcēṟṟu maṇṇip paḻanāḷil kaṭṭaṭap paṇikkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iṉṟum maturi māvaṭṭa ucilampaṭṭip pakutiyil attaku maṇvīṭukaḷik kāṇalām. nattamaṇ eṉpatu makkaḷ vaḻakku.
nantal 
nantutal  > nantal. nantu = caṅku.
	nantal 	= 	viḷaṅkutal, perukutal ataṟku etiriṭiyām keṭutal, curuṅkutal.
vīramiku maṉṉaṉivaṉ viṭṭārkkum naṭṭārkkum
tāraṇiyiṉ ākkiṉaṉan tal
naṭṭavarkku nantal ākkam; viṭṭavarkku nantal kēṭu.
nanti 
nantu  > nanti = viḷakkamikkatu; kāḷi.
kāḷiyiṉ naṭiyum timilum kaṇṭārik kavarvaṉa. cila kāḷikaḷiṉ kompum tāṭiyum kaṇṇiviṭṭu akalātavi.
tañciyiṉ nanti, karuvaṟikkup pōka niṉivāriyum kaṭṭip pōṭṭu niṟutta valla parumiyum perumiyu muṭiyatātal kaṇkūṭu.
nantu 
nantu = caṅku. nantutal = oḷiviṭutal.
oḷiviṭutal uṇmiyāl nantu eṉappaṭṭatu.
katirkkōṭṭu nantiṉ curimuka ēṟṟi	- puṟam. 266
nantu  > nattu  > natti. nattam eṉpatum atu.
nattampōl kēṭum uḷatākum cākkāṭum	- tiruk. 235
nappi 
īyāk karumiyi nappi eṉpatu nelli vaḻakkākum. nakkutal = virumpic cuvittal; naccutal = virumputal; nattutal = virumpik kiṭattal. ivaṟṟip pōṉṟa collamippu, napputal ākum. taṉakkē ellāmum eṉṉum taṟpaṟṟē atu. ātalāl īyāk karumiyāy eccil ki utirāṉāy iruppāṉi nappi eṉpatu vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. potuvaḻakku, ilakkiya vaḻakkil iḻantupōṉa vaḷatti mīṭṭeṭukka utavumeṉak kāṭṭum cāṉṟām itu.
namari 
kattiyi namari eṉpatu tiṇṭukkal vaṭṭāra vaḻakku. pēm nām urum accam eṉpatu tolkāppiya uriyiyal (848) nūṟpā. nām eṉpatu nam ātal colliyal muṟi. nam āki accapporuḷtarum kattiyik kuṟikkum vaḻakkāki irukkalām. amari, camari eṉpavaṟṟi namariyuṭaṉ vittu eṇṇip pārkkalām.
namaṉ 
camaṉ  > namaṉ.
evvuyirukkum oppavaṉāka iruntu kaṭaṉāṟṟum kālaṉ eṉpataṉ camaṉmi karutiya peyar. ñamaṉ eṉpatum atu.
camaṉkōl, namaṉkōl, ñamaṉkōl, cīrtūkkum kōl, niṟikōl eṉpaṉavellām oruporuḷaṉa.

nampikki 
nampum mēvum naciyā kummē
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (812). viruppattāl oṉṟiṉ mēl īṭupaṭutal, kaṭṭāyam velvēm eṉṉum nampikkiyi uṇṭākkum. nampikki illāmal īṭupaṭum ecceyalum, ettaṭiyum illāmal annampikki illāmiyē taṭiyāyt tāṉē tōṟṟup pōkac ceyyum. ātalāl en nampikkiyilum, taṉṉampikki iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
nampirāṉ 
nam + perumakaṉ  > perumāṉ  > pirāṉ.
iṟivaṉiyum iṟiyaṭiyāriyum āḷum aracaṉiyum nampirāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. itaṉ peṇpāl nampirāṭṭi. nam perumāṭṭi eṉpataṉ marūu. tampirāṉ tōḻar eṉpār cuntara mūrttiyār. nam eṉpatu urimic cuṭṭu.
* perumāṉ kāṇka
nammaṭṭi 
maṇveṭṭi eṉpatu, kocci vaḻakkil mampeṭṭi, mampaṭṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkutal potuvaḻakkākum. atu nammaṭṭi eṉa vaḻaṅkutal viḷavaṅkōṭu vaṭṭāra vaḻakkākum. itu, kocciyilum kocciyākum.
nayapparam 
nayapparam eṉpatu koḻuppu eṉṉum poruḷil maturic cilimāṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkāka uḷḷatu. tōṟṟap polivu taruvatāl koḻuppukku ippeyar ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām.
nayam payam 
	nayam	= 	nayantu allatu nayattāl payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷutal.
	payam	= 	payappaṭutti allatu accuṟuttip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷutal.
nayapayam kāṭṭi niṉittati niṟivēṟṟik koḷḷutal cilar iyaṟkiyām. āṭik kaṟappati āṭik kaṟattal, pāṭik kaṟappatip pāṭik kaṟattal pōl payaṉpaṭuttutal nayamām. vallāṇmi yālum cūḻccit tiṟattālum oruvaṉi accuṟuttit taṉceyali niṟivēṟṟik koḷḷutal payamām. ivviraṇṭum ulakiṭiyil kāṇak kūṭiyaṉavēyām.
narantam 
narantam eṉpatu oruvakip pul; appullit toṭṭa kiyil ataṉ maṇam kamaḻum! ataṉi meṉṟāl maṇamum iṉimiyum uḷḷami teḷivām.
maṇaney eṭukkap payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷuḷ narantam oṉṟu. atiyamāṉ kiyiṉ naṟumaṇam, narantam aṉṉatu eṉpataṉi auviyār,
narantam nāṟum taṉkiyāl
eṉkiṟār (puṟam. 235). naranta ney kūntalukkup pūcappaṭṭami,
narantam nāṟiruṅ kūntal
eṉpatu kalippā (54). narantap pulli māṉ virumpiyuṇṇum. eṉavē,
naranti naṟumpul mēyntu kavari
eṉkiṟatu puṟappāṭal (122).
narampu nāṟṟu 
narampu= narampu vittup pōṉa allatu muṟṟiya nāṟṟu.
nāṟṟu= naṭutaṟkuriya paruva niliyil amintuḷḷa nāṟṟu.
kāykaṟi tavaca vittukaḷi nāṟṟaṅkālil muḷikkac ceytu vaḷarttu uriya vaḷarcci niliyil naṭavu ceytal vēḷāṇmi muṟiyām. ammuṟiyil narampu vitta allatu maṭṭi vitta nāṟṟu muṟṟiyataṟkuc cāṉṟām. annāṟṟi naṭṭāl pakkam viriyātu. naṭṭatu maṭṭumē ciṉṉañciṟu katir vāṅki allatu payaṉ tantu niṉṟuviṭum. narampum nāṟṟumāka irukkiṟatu; oru pātiyē payaṉpaṭum eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu.
nāṟu  > nāṟṟu. nāṟutal = muḷittal.
naralutal 
olittal poruḷatu.
naral peruttuviṭṭatu eṉpatu nelli vaḻakku. naral = makkaḷ; avarkaḷ perukiya iṭattu uṇṭākum oli naralutal āyiṟṟu. muralutal eṉpatu kuḻalici yātal eṇṇat takkatu.
naravali 
naravali eṉpatu, narampuc cilantiyik kuṟikkum. naravali eṉṉum peyar accilanti pōṉṟa kalvirucam paḻattiṟku allatu mūkkuc caḷip paḻattiṟku vaṭārkkāṭṭup pāṅkaril vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu. (tēvanē. col. 13, 14).
nari 
nari eṉpatu veṇṇiṟam. nari niṟamuṭiya vilaṅku nari eṉappaṭṭatu. veṇṇiṟap pacu nariyāṉ eṉappaṭutal uṇṭu. narippuṟak kaḻuti (akam. 207) āyaṉ āṭṭi ōṭṭic celvatu pōl taṉ vālāl aṭittum vāyāl caṅkip piṭittum naṭakka vittuk koṇṭu pōkavallatu. ūḷi iṭuvatāl ataṉi ūḷi eṉṟum ūḷaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.
kālāḻ kaḷaril nariyaṭum kaṇṇañcā
vēlāḷ mukatta kaḷiṟu	- tiruk. 500
narimā eṉpatum itu.
iciyum eṉiṉum iciyā teṉiṉum
vacitīra eṇṇuvar cāṉṟōr - viciyiṉ
narimā uḷaṅkiḻitta ampiṉiṉ tītō
arimāp piḻippeyta kōl	- nālaṭi. 152
nariyaṉ 
nari + aṉ = nariyaṉ.
nari, cūḻccikku uvamiyāvatu. accūḻcci vañcamikka taṉṉalac cūḻcciyām. attakiya cūḻcciyaṉām. nariyaṉ eṉṉum vañcaṉ peyari narikkaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl pāvēntar vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. cūḻcciyil narikkaṇṇaṉ maṭṭumallaṉ, narikku aṇṇaṉum āvaṉ avaṉ. nūl pāṇṭiyaṉ paricu. nāṭaka uṟuppukku ēṟṟa nayattaku peyar.
naruḷ 
naruḷ = makkaḷ, kūṭṭam.
naralutal eṉpatu olittal. makkaḷ kūṭṭamākak kūṭiya iṭattil oli mikkiruttal veḷippaṭi. ātalāl, olittal poruḷtarum naral avvolikku aṭippaṭiyāka aminta kūṭṭatti naral eṉak kuṟittu naruḷ eṉṟākiyatu. naruḷ peruttup pōṉatu eṉpatil makkaḷ perukiviṭṭaṉar eṉṉum kuṟippuḷatu. ivvaḷavu patavalā? eṉpatum makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷē tarutalum vaḻakkē. pataval = kūṭṭam.
nari 
nuri  > nari.
pālnurip pōrvi pōrttu āṟuvarutal kaṇkūṭu.
mīṉuṇ kokkiṉ tūvi yaṉṉa
vālnari kūntal mutiyōḷ	- puṟam. 277
nalaṅku 
nalaṅku  > naluṅku.
maṇam ceytavaḷum, vayiṟu vāyttavaḷum ettuyarum uṟāmal ettīmiyum aṭiyāmal nalamāka vāḻavum nalamāka makappēṟu eytavum makaḷir nīrkkalam cuṟṟi vāḻttutal nalaṅku ākum. naluṅku eṉpatu   ma.va.
nalam 
nal + am = nalam.
nalam = naṉmiyāṉatu, maṅkalamāṉatu.
nalam x polam. pollātatu polam. enta nalattiṟkum muntu nalam, uṭalnalam. ātalāl uṭalnalattuṟi eṉat tuṟiyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu. xUtiu¡ f©lhš, ‘eykh? eṉa viṉavutal paṇpāṭu āyatu. viruppum kaṇṇōṭṭamum iṉpamum nalamām. iviyuḷḷa iṭam aḻaku uṟiyuḷ eṉaṟku aiyamiṉṟē! ākaliṉ nalamum aḻakeṉalāyiṟṟām.
nalam puṉinturittal tirukkuṟaḷiṉ (112) ōratikārap peyar. nalam pārāṭṭal, mēlum mēlum nalamākac ceyya vikkum.
nalam polam 
	nalam	=	pūppu nīrāṭṭu maṇam pōṉṟa naṉṉikaḻccikaḷ.
	polam	= 	nōy iṟappu pōṉṟa tīya nikaḻccikaḷ.
nallatu pollatu eṉpatum ituvē. uṟṟār uṟavāka iruntum pakiyāki iruppārum, koṇṭu koṭuttal illāta ayalārāka iruppārum kūṭa, ōrūril nikaḻum polaṅkaḷit taḷḷi vikka māṭṭār. ūroḻuṅku atuvākum. avvāṟu ceytu ūrppakiyittēṭik koḷḷa evarum virumpār. yārāka iruntālum nalampolam taḷḷalāmā? eṉpatu ciṟṟūr vaḻakku.
nalital 
nalital = nalattil kuṟital nalitalām.
uṭalnili poruḷnili muyaṟcinili āyavaṟṟil kuṟivu uṇṭātal nalla niliyil iruntu nalivu niliyuṟutalākavē karutappaṭum. nalital, melital vaṟumi, tōlvi iḻappu āyavaṟṟāl uṇṭātal evarum aṟintatu.
naliviṉmēl nalivātalum kaṇkūṭu. āṉāl, naṉmuyaṟci yāḷaṉ oruvaṉ kuṭiyil piṟantāl nalivaṉittum tūḷākip pōtal uṟuti.
eṇṇiya eṇṇiyāṅ keytupa eṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiyar ākap peṟiṉ	- tiruk. 666
icicceyalkaḷ eṭṭaṉuḷ nalital oṉṟu eṉpār aṭiyārkku nallār.
eṭuttal, paṭuttal, nalital, kampitam, kuṭilam, oli, uruṭṭu, tākku eṉpavi (cilap. 3:26) avi.
nalkutal 
koṭuttal eṉpatu poruḷ. etik koṭuttal nalkutal? nallatik koṭuttalē nalkutalām. vāḻvukku iṉṟiyamiyātatik kaṭṭāyam tēvippaṭuvārkkut tarutalē nalkutalām.
palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nalkutal nōkkiyē naṭipeṟa vēṇṭiyuḷatu. toḻilāḷar āṇṭuḻippukku nalkuki peṟutal urimiyāyiṟṟu. an nalkukiyi naṉṉar eṉpār pāvāṇar.
nalkutalāl payaṉuṇṭāyiṉ nalkuki nalkukiyēyām!
nalkūrtal 
 nalku + uravu = nalkuravu  > nalkūrtal = vaṟumi.
nalku = naṉmi. naṉmi eṉṟiruppavi ūrntu viṭutal, nalku ūrtal ākum. naṉmiyāyiṉavi ellām akaṉṟuviṭṭa tuyarnili. vaṟumi tuṉpamē eṉiṉum attuṉpamē māntar vāḻvukkum uyarvukkum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
vaṟumiyē uḻippiṉ untunili; vaṟumiyāl ēṟpaṭum vayiṟṟaṭippāṭu ettuyariyum tāṅkum uḷavalimi uṭal valimi uḷanalam uṭalnalam eṉpavaṟṟi avarkku ūṭṭiṉum, ulaka nalattukku vēṇṭum kaṭiya uḻippu ayarā uḻippu āyavaṟṟi ōyātu kiḷarac ceyvatāl ulaka nalamum ām. ivvaṭipporuḷ koṇṭē nalkūrtalum nalkuravum amintaṉavām. nalkuravu tirukkuṟaḷil (105) ōr atikārap peyar.
nallaṭakkam 
aṭakkam eṉpatu ōr uyariya paṇpu. nallaṭakkam eṉṉum iccol iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷip putippatik kuṟikkiṟatu. aṭakkam ceytal eṉṉum atu, īḻattil nallaṭakkam eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kurutiyōṭṭam, mūccu mutaliyavi aṭaṅkiviṭṭa uṭalip peṭṭiyil kallil maṇṇil mūṭip putippatu ātalāl appeyar peṟṟatu. iṟantār māṇṭār eṉpavi ciṟantār eṉṉum poruḷaṉa. avari aṭakkam ceytali nal eṉa aṭi tantu vaḻaṅkal pōṟṟutaṟkuriyatām.
nallatu naḷiyatu:
	nallatu	= 	kōyilil nikaḻum poṅkalviḻā tērttiruviḻā mutaliyaṉa.
	naḷiyatu	= 	kōyilil nikaḻum kuḷumi corital viḻā.
naṉmiyāvatu maṅkalam; maṅkala viḻākkaḷ nallatu eṉappaṭum. veppam mikkum, maḻi illātum, ammi mutaliya nōy vantum tuṉpuṟuttum kālattil teyvattiṟkuc cānti ceyya vēṇṭum eṉak kuḷumi corital eṉa oru viḻāk koṇṭāṭappaṭum. kuḷumi corital viḻāvē naḷiyatu ākum. naḷi eṉpatu kuḷircciyām. kuḷir naḷukkutal eṉpatu naṭukkutal poruḷil vantatu. kuḷir naṭukkuvatākaliṉ.
